      <div>

         <div><a name="xx_9c3d22bc-f849-4565-b062-9403eba8f82e_PartF-Header-SOI-3414_3"></a>

            <div style="page-break-after:always;position:relative;">

               <div style="clear:both;"> </div>

               <div style="float:left;margin-left:18pt;margin-top:21.5pt;width:559pt;min-height:24pt;">

                  <div style="line-height:12.00pt;margin-top:0.0pt;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:14pt;line-height:14.0pt;">Schedule of Investments </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;line-height:14.0pt;">(unaudited)&#8199;(continued)</font>

                     <div> </div><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;line-height:12.00pt;">August 31, 2026</font></div>
               </div>

               <div style="clear:both;"> </div>

               <div style="float:left;margin-left:18pt;margin-top:-21.0pt;width:559pt;min-height:24pt;">

                  <div style="line-height:10.0pt;margin-top:0.0pt;text-align:right;"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:10pt;font-weight:bold;line-height:10.0pt;">BlackRock Technology Opportunities Fund</font></div>
               </div>

               <div style="clear:both;"> </div>

               <div style="float:left;margin-left:18pt;margin-top:17.5pt;width:558.31pt;min-height:668pt;">

                  <div style="line-height:11.00pt;margin-top:0.00pt;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;font-weight:bold;">Fair Value Hierarchy as of Period End</font></div>

                  <div style="line-height:11.00pt;margin-top:8.00pt;text-align:justify;">

                     <div style="margin-top:8pt;"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;margin-left:0%;">Various inputs are used in determining the fair value of financial instruments at
                           the measurement date. These inputs to valuation techniques are categorized into a
                           fair value </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">hierarchy consisting of three broad levels for financial reporting purposes as follows:</font></div>
                  </div>

                  <div style="line-height:11.00pt;margin-left:9pt;margin-top:9.00pt;text-align:left;">

                     <div style="margin-top:9pt;"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;line-height:11.00pt;">&#8195;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">Level 1 &#8212; Unadjusted price quotations in active markets/exchanges that the Fund has the ability to access for identical assets or liabilities;</font></div>
                  </div>

                  <div style="line-height:11.00pt;margin-left:9pt;margin-top:9.00pt;text-align:left;">

                     <div style="margin-top:9pt;"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;line-height:11.00pt;">&#8195;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">Level 2 &#8212; Inputs other than quoted prices included within Level 1 that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly or indirectly; and</font></div>
                  </div>

                  <div style="line-height:11.00pt;margin-left:18.87pt;margin-top:9.00pt;text-align:justify;">

                     <div style="margin-top:9pt;"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;margin-left:-1.77%;">&#8226;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;line-height:11.00pt;">&#8195;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">Level 3 &#8212; Inputs that are unobservable and significant to the entire fair value measurement for the asset or liability (including the Valuation Committee&#8217;s assumptions </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">used in determining the fair value of financial instruments).</font></div>
                  </div>

                  <div style="line-height:11.00pt;margin-top:8.00pt;text-align:justify;">

                     <div style="margin-top:8pt;"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;margin-left:0%;">The hierarchy gives the highest priority to unadjusted quoted prices in active markets
                           for identical assets or liabilities (Level 1 measurements) and the lowest priority
                           to </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;margin-left:0%;">unobservable inputs (Level 3 measurements). Accordingly, the degree of judgment exercised
                           in determining fair value is greatest for instruments categorized in Level 3. The
                           </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;margin-left:0%;">inputs used to measure fair value may fall into different levels of the fair value
                           hierarchy. In such cases, for disclosure purposes, the fair value hierarchy classification
                           is </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;margin-left:0%;">determined based on the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value measurement
                           in its entirety. Investments classified within Level 3 have significant unobservable
                           </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;margin-left:0%;">inputs used by the Valuation Committee in determining the price for Fair Valued Investments.
                           Level 3 investments include equity or debt issued by privately held companies or </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;margin-left:0%;">funds that may not have a secondary market and/or may have a limited number of investors.
                           The categorization of a value determined for financial instruments is based on the
                           </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;margin-left:0%;">pricing transparency of the financial instruments and is not necessarily an indication of the risks associated with investing in those securities. For information about the Fund&#8217;s </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">policy regarding valuation of financial instruments, refer to its most recent financial
                           statements.</font></div>
                  </div>

                  <div style="line-height:11.00pt;margin-top:8.00pt;text-align:left;">

                     <div style="margin-top:8pt;"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">The following table summarizes the Fund</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">&#8217;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">s financial instruments categorized in the fair value hierarchy. The breakdown of
                           the Fund</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">&#8217;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">s financial instruments into major </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:9pt;">categories is disclosed in the Schedule of Investments above.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:Arial Narrow;font-size:1pt;line-height:1.00pt;">&#8195;</font></div>
                  </div>

                  <div style="line-height:1.00pt;margin-top:-1pt;text-align:left;"> </div>
